The audit's one nominally significant interaction, $+0.378$ ($p = 0.002$), is not identified as preference: a construction containing zero differential preference reproduces 79 to 85\% of it from the observed severity shift and the scale floor alone. We derive the mechanism in closed form and show that its contribution is measurable from an audit's own ratings.

</details>


### [105] [BTS-AgentBench: A Deterministic, Replayable Pipeline from Read-Only Telemetry Logs to Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27334)
*Jeong-Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从工业遥测数据构建可执行多轮智能体任务的方法，并发布了 BTS-AgentBench 基准。该方法将原始遥测记录标准化为只读工具库，编译带有工具派生的金标准答案和证据的静态任务，再将保留任务提升为类型化、有界的操作员面向情节。基准包含 532 行数据，具有澄清、目标修订、时间戳策略、质量门控报告和证据归因等特性，且通过了代码契约预检和构建-排除控制器验证。在 XAI4HEAT 数据集上复现得到 204 个情节，保留的 GPT-5.5 执行在 41 行保留测试集上全部通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场所积累了大量只读遥测数据，但缺乏将这些记录编译为可执行多轮智能体任务的基准规范。现有基准难以直接利用真实工业遥测数据构建高质量、可复现的智能体评测环境。

Method: 提出遥测到情节的构建流水线：(1) 将 BTS 元数据和原始历史标准化为只读工具库；(2) 编译静态任务，配备工具派生的金标准答案和证据；(3) 将保留任务提升为类型化、有界的操作员面向情节，引入澄清、目标修订、时间戳策略、质量门控报告和证据归因机制。采用代码契约预检和构建-排除控制器验证构建质量，并通过两次独立原始到情节构建验证复现性。

Result: 发布 532 行 BTS-AgentBench 基准，代码契约预检零发现，构建-排除控制器完成 0/532 行；两次独立构建匹配所有 11 个逻辑工具库导出，精确复现 356/87/89 训练/开发/测试划分。将相同构建路径应用于 XAI4HEAT 产生 204 个情节，其 41 行保留测试集上控制器完成 0 行，保留的 GPT-5.5 执行完成全部 41 行。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从工业遥测数据到高质量、可复现智能体基准的自动化构建，验证了构建流程的鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化能力。发布的基准、代码和复现报告为工业智能体评测提供了可靠基础设施。

Abstract: Industrial sites contain large volumes of read-only telemetry, but few benchmarks specify how to compile these records into executable multi-turn agent tasks. We present a telemetry-to-episode construction method instantiated as BTS-AgentBench. The pipeline normalizes BTS metadata and raw histories into a read-only tool store, compiles static tasks with tool-derived gold answers and evidence, and lifts retained tasks into typed, bounded operator-facing episodes. The 532-row release adds clarification, goal revision, timestamp policy, quality-gated reporting, and evidence attribution while preserving the source computation and split. Coded contract preflight reports zero findings, and the construction-exclusion controller completes 0/532 rows. Two independent raw-to-episode builds match all 11 logical tool-store exports and reproduce the released 356/87/89 train/dev/test artifact exactly. Applying the shared construction path to XAI4HEAT produces 204 episodes; on its 41-row held-out test split, the controller completes 0 rows and the retained GPT-5.5 execution completes all 41. Code, artifacts, and replay reports are available at https://github.com/kjy7567/BTS-AgentBench.

</details>


### [106] [Puro-2B: Poor Lab's Qwen2-1.5B Trained on RTX 5090 within $5090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27370)
*Kairong Luo,Jiarui Cui,Yaorui Yin,Shengqi Chen,Yiming Yang,Linxiang Gao,Yanmohan Wang,Mingzhe Zhang,Kaiyue Wen,Kaifeng Lyu,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Puro-2B开放预训练配方，在消费级RTX 5090 GPU上使用FP8精度从头训练2B参数模型，成本低于$6.9K，性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B。同时推导出Puro成本缩放定律，显示约$4.4K即可达到Qwen2-1.5B性能，并研究预训练数据课程对后训练下游性能的影响。全套配方（数据、代码、权重）已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练成本高昂（甚至小模型需百万级美元），导致学术界和开源社区难以承担。现有开源努力（开放权重模型、训练配方）仍缺乏低成本、硬件可及、完全开源的预训练配方。

Method: 综合多种方法：1) 硬件选择——消费级RTX 5090 GPU；2) 低精度训练——FP8精度；3) Hyperball优化；4) 课程模型平均；5) 数据配方设计。在1.4万亿tokens上从头训练Puro-2B模型集合，探索不同token预算和配方变体。

Result: 1) 最佳模型训练成本<$6.9K，性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B；2) 推导Puro成本缩放定律，拟合显示约$4.4K（<$5,090）可达Qwen2-1.5B性能；3) 通过全流程控制研究，揭示预训练数据课程如何塑造后训练下游性能。

Conclusion: 发布完整开源预训练配方（Apache 2.0），大幅降低预训练门槛，使学术界和开源社区能在消费级硬件上低成本复现。成本缩放定律和课程学习研究为未来高效预训练提供理论指导和实证依据。

Abstract: Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. Although strong open-source efforts already exist, including open-weight models and open-source training recipes, a cost-efficient, hardware-accessible, and open-source pretraining recipe has long been missing. Even at a small scale, training Llama-3.2-3B costs over \$1.5M, and reproducing SmolLM3-3B needs over \$700K. In this report, we present an open pretraining recipe designed to lower this barrier. Using this recipe, we train a collection of Puro-2B models from scratch on up to 1.4 trillion tokens with FP8 precision on consumer-grade RTX 5090 GPUs. The models in the collection differ in token budgets and selected recipe variants. Our best model is trained at a compute cost of less than \$6.9K and approaches Qwen2.5-1.5B performance under our evaluation protocol. This cost efficiency is enabled by a combination of approaches, including hardware selection, low-precision training, hyperball optimization, curriculum model averaging, and the data recipe. Beyond the recipe itself, we provide two additional results. First, across the Puro-2B collection, we derive a Puro Cost Scaling Law that relates training cost to average model performance; the fitted law suggests that about \$4.4K, less than \$5,090, is sufficient to reach the performance of Qwen2-1.5B. Second, as an end-to-end case study, we examine how pretraining data curricula shape downstream performance after post-training. Such controlled studies are enabled by having access to the full pretraining pipeline rather than model weights alone. We release the full training recipe for Puro-2B, including data, code, and model weights under Apache 2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/thu-pacman/puro-2b.

</details>


### [107] [Pair-Level Essay-Scale Republication and Reuse from Fragmented Historical Text Reuse: A Workflow Study on Eighteenth-Century Books and Newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27343)
*Ke Shu,Kira Hinderks,Eetu Mäkelä,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从碎片化的文本复用证据中恢复论文规模的再版与复用，核心挑战是成对证据的整合而非片段检索。以18世纪苏格兰哲学家大卫·休谟的论文为候选集，涵盖ECCO书籍和历史报纸。将任务建模为成对证据整合为合理的传播关系，对比了分阶段基于规则的工作流、基线方法（决策树和两种直接LLM设置）及自动化规则适应三种方法族。实验显示，特征聚合即可在标注的ECCO-ECCO切片上达到0.948 F1，最终工作流在精确率-召回率权衡上表现最佳。在完整ECCO-ECCO候选空间中，直接LLM基线标记多达14,886对再版，而最终工作流仅771对，LLM表现为高召回候选扩展器而非精确控制的分类器。在ECCO-报纸上，人工审核确认所有176个预测正例均为真实再版或复用，揭示了额外的来源结构。在真值不完整情况下，可审计的成对证据整合为历史考察提供了紧凑的候选空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本复用检测多关注片段级检索，但论文规模的再版与复用恢复需要从碎片化证据中整合成对级的传播关系。由于输入是碎片化的复用命中而非干净的文档对，且正样本覆盖天然不完整，传统方法难以直接应用。因此需要一种可审计的成对证据整合方法，为历史学研究提供紧凑且可靠的候选空间。

Method: 将任务形式化为成对证据整合为合理的传播关系，设计并对比三种方法族：1) 分阶段基于规则的工作流，通过特征聚合和规则逐步筛选；2) 基线方法，包括决策树和两种直接提示LLM的设置；3) 自动化规则适应。在标注的ECCO-ECCO切片和完整候选宇宙上评估，并在ECCO-报纸候选集上进行人工审核。

Result: 在标注的ECCO-ECCO主切片上，仅成对特征聚合即达0.948 F1，最终工作流在测试的规则阶段中实现最优精确率-召回率权衡。在完整ECCO-ECCO候选宇宙中，直接LLM基线标记高达14,886对再版，远多于最终工作流的771对，LLM在此直接提示设置下表现为高召回候选扩展器而非精确控制的部署分类器。在ECCO-报纸任务上，人工审核确认所有176个预测正例均为真实再版或复用，且发现版面重复和源端多重性揭示了额外的溯源结构。

Conclusion: 在真值不完整的情况下，可审计的成对证据整合提供了一种实用的方法，能生成紧凑的候选空间供历史学专家进一步考察，优于单纯依赖高召回但低精确的LLM直接预测或简单规则。

Abstract: This paper addresses the recovery of essay-scale republication and reuse from fragmented text-reuse evidence, a setting whose central challenge is pair-level evidence consolidation and not fragment retrieval alone. The study focuses on a candidate set centered on essays by eighteenth-century Scottish philosopher David Hume, spanning books from ECCO (Eighteenth Century Collections Online) and historical newspapers. Because the input consists of fragmented reuse hits instead of clean document pairs, and positive coverage is inherently incomplete, we formulate the task as pair-level evidence consolidation into plausible transmission relations and compare three methodological families: a staged rule-based workflow, baselines (a decision tree and two direct LLM settings), and automated rule adaptation. On labeled ECCO--ECCO slices, pair-level feature aggregation alone already reaches 0.948 F1 on the main labeled slice, while the final workflow gives the strongest overall precision-recall trade-off among the tested rule stages. On the full ECCO--ECCO candidate universe, direct LLM baselines flag up to 14,886 pairs as reprints compared to 771 for the final workflow, behaving in this direct-prompt setup as high-recall candidate expanders rather than precision-controlled deployment classifiers. On ECCO--Newspaper, manual audit confirms all 176 predicted positives as genuine cases of republication or reuse, while issue duplication and source-side multiplicity reveal additional provenance structure. Under incomplete ground truth, auditable pair-level evidence consolidation provides a practical way to produce compact candidate spaces for historical inspection.

</details>


### [108] [INTENT-AS-A-TOOL Makes it Easy to Track Agentic Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27348)
*Yutong Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Peng Xu,Long Wang,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaoping Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出INTENT-AS-A-TOOL方法，通过引入意图导向工具为LLM智能体提供专用表达通道，利用工具调用概率作为无需评判器的细粒度信号，实时追踪推理过程中的有害意图演变，补充CoT监控并支持在线干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM作为自主智能体部署，安全失效越来越涉及实质性行动。研究聚焦‘智能体错位’——智能体在目标冲突与压力下采取有害行动。CoT监控虽发现有害执行前存在意图信号，但事后CoT标签过于粗糙，无法展示生成过程中意图如何动态变化。

Method: 引入INTENT-AS-A-TOOL：为模型添加意图导向工具，提供表达对目标行为承诺的专用通道。工具调用概率直接反映模型追求该行为的倾向，无需外部评判器即可获得细粒度意图信号。

Result: 实验表明INTENT-AS-A-TOOL能有效补充CoT监控，将事后粗糙的CoT标签扩展为密集的意图轨迹，并能识别适合在线干预的关键推理步骤。

Conclusion: 行动偏好可作为推理过程中追踪智能体错位的有效信号。代码与数据已开源：https://github.com/RebeccaZhang22/intent-as-a-tool

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous agents, safety failures increasingly involve consequential actions. We study agentic misalignment, where agents take harmful actions under goal conflicts and pressures. Using chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring, we find that harmful execution is often preceded by intent signals in reasoning. However, post-hoc CoT labels are too coarse to show how intent changes during generation. We introduce INTENT-AS-A-TOOL, an approach that adds intent-targeted tools to give the model a dedicated channel for expressing commitment to a target behavior. The probability of calling an intent tool provides a judge-free, fine-grained signal of the model's tendency to pursue that behavior. Our results show that INTENT-AS-A-TOOL complements CoT monitoring, expands post-hoc CoT labels into dense trajectories, and identifies critical steps for online intervention. These findings suggest that action preferences are useful for tracking agentic misalignment during reasoning. Our code and data are accessible: https://github.com/RebeccaZhang22/intent-as-a-tool.

</details>


### [109] [How Language Models Organize and Structure Moral Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27402)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过线性探针分析大语言模型中道德知识的几何组织结构，发现道德基础方向在表示空间中既相互独立又共享共同成分，模型表征的是道德张力而非预先解决的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否仅检测道德内容，还是进一步区分不同道德基础并以几何方式组织它们之间的关系。

Method: 在开放权重语言模型上为每个道德基础理论（MFT）类别训练六个独立的线性探针，检查由此产生的方向在表示空间中的相互关系。

Result: 道德基础方向既不坍缩为单一探测器也不相互隔离，而是跨越近似最大数量的独立维度并共享一个正的公共成分（平均余弦相似度0.26，远高于非道德概念的0.013）。这种几何结构跨架构和规模一致，在预训练早期形成。结构未支持MFT的个体化/约束区分，反而反映语料库统计。道德困境方向部分由组成基础构成（2.7倍基线），大部分方差编码冲突特有结构。

Conclusion: 大语言模型将道德知识组织为一个整合的几何空间，其中道德基础既保持区分性又共享核心成分，模型表征道德张力本身而非预先确定的判断。

Abstract: How do large language models (LLMs) organize moral knowledge? Models detect moral content broadly, but detection is a low bar. We ask whether they go further, distinguishing moral foundations from one another and organizing the relationships between them geometrically.
  We train six independent linear probes on open-weight language models, one per Moral Foundations Theory (MFT) category (care/harm, fair/cheat, lib/oppress, loy/betray, auth/subv, sanc/degrade), and examine how the resulting directions relate to each other in representation space. We find the directions neither collapse into a single moral detector nor isolate from one another. Rather, they span a near-maximal number of independent dimensions while sharing a positive common component. The shared component is the signature of integration, and it is moral-specific relative to a matched non-moral concept battery built identically (mean pairwise cosine 0.26 vs. 0.013).
  The geometry is consistent across architectures and scale and reaches its integration regime early in pre-training, well before probe accuracy saturates. The structure the model discovers shows no evidence of the individualizing/binding distinction predicted by Moral Foundations Theory (an underpowered test: only 20 candidate partitions exist) but rather reflects corpus statistics. Extending to moral dilemmas, each dilemma direction partially composes from its component foundations, at 2.7x a mismatched-pair baseline, while the majority of its variance encodes conflict-specific structure. The model represents moral tension itself, not a pre-resolved judgment.

</details>


### [110] [RCMN: Understanding Misleadingness in Influential Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27358)
*Peiling Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reader-Centric Misleadingness Understanding (RCMN)框架，从误导机制、读者解读、证据支持解读、情绪唤起、传播意图五个维度操作化误导性，构建证据支撑的公共话语数据集，发现误导形式多样（非仅捏造），且常伴随高情绪唤起和扭曲意图；轻量级声明-上下文表示能较好恢复读者层面解读，但识别误导生成机制仍需更丰富的语境与证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少关注公共话语如何通过框架、省略、语境化等手段产生误导并塑造读者解读，缺乏以读者为中心、多维度的误导性理解框架与实证分析。

Method: 1. 提出RCMN框架，定义五大维度操作化误导性；2. 基于框架构建证据支撑的有影响力公共话语数据集；3. 实证分析误导机制分布及其与情绪唤起、传播意图的关联；4. 评估轻量级声明-上下文表示在五个生成式基础模型上恢复读者解读与识别误导机制的能力。

Result: 误导性远超捏造，无根据推断、夸大、遗漏等机制普遍，常伴随高情绪唤起和扭曲传播意图；轻量级表示能较好恢复读者层面解读，但识别误导生成机制显著更难，需更丰富的语境与证据信息。

Conclusion: 轻量级声明-上下文表示具备可扩展误导性分析的潜力，但可靠理解误导生成机制仍依赖更充分的语境、证据与多模态信息支撑。

Abstract: Influential public discourse shapes public beliefs and can also mislead, not only through what is stated, but also through how information is framed, omitted, contextualised, and communicated. Yet less research has focused on how such misleadingness arises and shapes the interpretations formed by readers. To address this gap, we introduce Reader-Centric Misleadingness Understanding (RCMN), a framework that operationalises misleadingness through five dimensions: misleading mechanism, likely reader interpretation, evidence-warranted interpretation, emotional arousal, and communicative intent. Based on this framework, we construct an evidence-grounded dataset of influential public discourse. Empirical findings show that misleadingness is diverse and extends well beyond fabrication, with unsupported inference, exaggeration, and omission among the prevalent mechanisms, and is frequently associated with heightened emotional arousal and distortive communicative intent. Moreover, we investigate whether lightweight claim-and-context representations retain sufficient cues for understanding reader-centric misleadingness without access to richer contextual, evidential, and multimodal information. Evaluation across five recent generative foundation models shows that reader-level interpretations can often be recovered from such limited representations, whereas identifying how misleadingness is produced remains considerably more challenging. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight representations for scalable misleadingness analysis, while reliable understanding of misleading mechanisms continues to require richer contextual and evidential grounding.

</details>


### [111] [Your Voice Cloning System is Secretly a Voice Anonymizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27360)
*Romolo Muletta,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出将多语言语音克隆模型XTTSv2（在27k小时语音上训练）重新用于说话人匿名化，无需重新训练，通过伪说话人条件和迭代优化策略，在7种欧洲语言上实现近似最优隐私保护（EER≈0.49）、有竞争力的可懂度和显著更好的语音质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话人匿名化需要抑制说话人身份特征同时保留语言内容和语音质量。现有专用匿名化方法通常需要语言特定的训练或重新训练，限制了其跨语言适用性和部署效率。

Method: 利用XTTSv2的声音克隆能力，通过条件化伪说话人实现语音转换，保留韵律结构独立于说话人身份。引入迭代优化策略，通过最大化说话人不相似性和可懂度的调和均值来平衡隐私和效用。

Result: 在CommonVoice和Multilingual LibriSpeech的7种欧洲语言评测中，系统达到近似最优隐私（EER≈0.49）、有竞争力的可懂度，且语音质量显著优于专用匿名化基线，且无需任何语言特定训练。

Conclusion: XTTSv2等大规模多语言语音克隆模型可被有效重新用于说话人匿名化任务，无需重新训练即可提供跨语言的强基线，展示了预训练生成模型在隐私保护语音处理中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Speaker anonymization suppresses speaker-identifying attributes from speech while preserving linguistic content and quality. We propose repurposing XTTSv2, a multilingual voice cloning model trained on 27k hours of speech, for speaker anonymization without retraining. Our key insight is that XTTSv2's voice cloning capabilities preserve prosodic structure independently of speaker identity, enabling voice conversion by conditioning on a pseudo-speaker. We introduce an iterative refinement strategy that balances privacy and utility by maximizing a harmonic mean of speaker dissimilarity and intelligibility. Evaluated on seven European languages across CommonVoice and Multilingual LibriSpeech, our system achieves near-optimal privacy (EER $\approx$ 0.49), competitive intelligibility, and substantially better speech quality than dedicated anonymization baselines, while requiring no language-specific training. We release the code here: https://github.com/rm00cr/coqui-tts.

</details>


### [112] [D2C-Routing: Dimension-to-Composition Evidence Routing for Mixed-Origin AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27380)
*Xin Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Yiqi Tong,Wei Guo,Yutian Xiao,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出D2C-Routing框架，将混合来源文本检测建模为维度到组合的来源归因任务，通过路由内容侧与表达侧证据到监督维度头，再经门控组合层预测四类协作标签，在MixD2C基准上达到0.8603四分类Avg TPR@1%FPR，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测多为二分类文档级判断，无法处理内容来源与表达来源不一致的混合来源写作场景，需细粒度区分内容侧与表达侧来源并组合为四类协作类型。

Method: 提出Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing)：先将文本编码为内容侧与表达侧证据，分别路由至监督的内容维度头与表达维度头，再通过学习到的门控组合层融合两维度预测，输出四分类协作标签（人-人、人-AI、AI-人、AI-AI）。

Result: 在从HART重构的MixD2C分割上，D2C-Routing系统达到0.8603四分类Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同分割下的RACE-local重跑基线高6.5个百分点；消融实验验证路由设计有效性；错误分析显示AI内容/人表达与全AI文本的边界最难区分。

Conclusion: D2C-Routing通过显式建模内容与表达两个维度并学习组合，有效解决混合来源文本检测问题，显著提升细粒度归因性能，但AI内容/人表达与纯AI文本的区分仍具挑战，未来可探索更强的表达侧特征建模。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [113] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAST框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取可审计的临床概念特征，抑制模板等伪影特征，在MIMIC-IV死亡率预测任务上超越微调编码器基线且与强LLM基线竞争，同时提供特征级审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在院内表现强但部署时因利用笔记特有伪影（模板、分隔符、样板文本）而失效，需开发可审计、鲁棒的临床文本分类方法。

Method: 1. 用SAE从Transformer中间激活中暴露稀疏、人类可审计的特征；2. 通过LLM辅助解释管道和ICD-10检索约束标注SAE潜变量；3. 微调时通过残差减法抑制经验证的伪影潜变量；4. 提供事后逐概念归因以审计模型决策。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院小结死亡率预测上，CAST优于对应微调编码器基线，与强LLM基线竞争，并产出支持预测的临床概念及训练中被抑制伪影概念的特征级审计轨迹。

Conclusion: CAST通过概念引导的伪影抑制实现了可审计的临床文本分类，在保持预测性能的同时提供透明的特征级解释，解决了模型对部署漂移敏感的问题。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [114] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种融合范式（Merge、Mix RL、MOPD）在多领域大语言模型能力整合中的表现，发现三者平均性能差异≤1.4分，但单一基准上可达8.6分差距；揭示了各范式的训练动态约束，并给出了基于场景的实用选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升大模型特定能力，但覆盖多能力常需训练领域专家再整合。现有三种融合范式（Merge合并任务向量、Mix RL混合数据集、MOPD双教师蒸馏）多孤立研究，缺乏系统对比与选择指导。

Method: 使用共享专家与数据，跨模型规模与多领域基准套件，系统对比Merge、Mix RL、MOPD三种融合范式；分析任务向量几何、训练动态、单样本准确率、解覆盖率及保留能力等指标。

Result: 三者平均性能差≤1.4分，单基准差距达8.6分，领域级差异反映任务向量几何中的跨域关系。Mix RL受领域混合比例约束，MOPD受教师模型上界限制，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为单一更新。三者均提升单样本准确率，但无显著解覆盖率增益或保留能力损失。

Conclusion: 实用指南：专家已存在且追求低成本融合选Merge；无专家需训练统一模型且可调领域比例促跨域迁移选Mix RL；重视保留领域特定增益、不必超越教师或最小化端到端成本选MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [115] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种简单有效的方法，通过引入较小弱模型生成的部分推理轨迹作为前缀，来缓解RLVR训练中的熵崩塌问题，显著扩展推理覆盖范围，且无需额外SFT、复杂奖励设计或提示工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能显著提升LLM推理能力，但常导致策略熵下降，造成推理覆盖面窄、大k值下pass@k性能下降。现有方法主要通过算法正则化缓解，但忽视了跨模型非参数扰动的潜力。

Method: 强制目标模型基于较小弱语言模型生成的部分推理轨迹（外部前缀）生成答案。这些陌生前缀能有效打破过度自信，鼓励探索不同推理路径。实证研究外部前缀的潜力，揭示分布差异对RLVR训练中探索动态的影响机制。

Result: 在多数学基准上，该方法持续优于原版RLVR。性能增益随k值增大而更加显著，展示出推理覆盖范围的实质性扩展。有效缓解熵崩塌，且无需额外SFT、复杂奖励设计或提示工程。

Conclusion: 通过引入弱模型生成的外部前缀，该方法为RLVR训练提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的熵保持策略，从根本上拓宽了模型的推理多样性，为大规模推理任务提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [116] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无放回重采样的无偏估计方法，用于计算转导语言模型中目标前缀概率，解决了阈值剪枝束和搜索产生的下界误差未知问题，并在文本和DNA任务上显著提升了计算效率和估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转导语言模型需要将源语言模型与有限状态转导器组合，计算目标前缀概率需对所有映射到该前缀的源字符串概率求和，但该集合可能指数级大或无限。现有方法基于源前缀概率的计算捷径并用阈值剪枝束和近似，仅能得到误差未知的下界，缺乏理论保证且计算开销大。

Method: 提出无放回重采样并按纳入概率逆加权的递归修正方法，得到目标前缀概率的无偏估计量，并能估计阈值剪枝损失的概率质量。束和算法扩展保留的源前缀并采样保留哪些前缀，随着累积概率质量增加减少保留前缀数量，保证算法以概率一终止。

Result: 在百科文本和DNA任务上，优于基于有放回重采样的序列蒙特卡洛基线：文本上实现更好的计算-方差权衡，DNA上在相同最大粒子数下误差更低。DNA到氨基酸转导任务中，运行时间比阈值剪枝束和降低几个数量级，使长目标字符串前缀概率估计成为可能。在已发布的阅读时间分析中替换阈值剪枝显著降低语料库惊讶度估计，但不改变原有结论。

Conclusion: 无偏采样替代阈值剪枝为转导语言模型提供了理论可靠且计算高效的前缀概率估计方法，在保持下游任务结论不变的前提下显著提升估计精度和运行效率。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [117] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO)，一种无需真实标签的测试时训练方法，通过不对称目标利用多数投票伪标签：对一致的轨迹进行 On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 蒸馏，对不一致的轨迹使用 Grouped RL 惩罚，并结合 Token 级选择机制，在五个竞赛级基准上达到有标签监督的 OPSD 水平，显著提升 Qwen3-1.7B 的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（RL、OPSD）依赖 Ground-truth 标签，无法进行测试时训练 (TTT)。用多数投票伪标签替代真实标签虽然自然，但脆弱：错误的投票会污染教师模型并误导所有 Token。观察到该失效模式是不对称的——与伪标签不一致的轨迹通常是错误的，无论投票本身是否正确。

Method: 提出 TTPO，包含不对称目标：1) 对与伪标签一致的轨迹应用 OPSD 进行蒸馏；2) 对不一致的轨迹使用 Grouped RL 进行惩罚。Token 级选择进一步优化：蒸馏分支降低已收敛位置的权重，RL 分支仅惩罚高置信度的错误。多数投票路由随着模型改进提供更紧密的自我监督。

Result: 在无任何标签情况下，TTPO 在五个竞赛级数学基准上匹配有标签监督的 OPSD 性能；将 Qwen3-1.7B 在 TTT 设置下的准确率从 38.0% 提升至 45.2%；在无思考模式下带来 +25.2% 到 +36.4% 的增益；展现出强跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: TTPO 通过不对称目标和 Token 级选择，有效解决了伪标签噪声导致的教师污染问题，实现了无标签的测试时策略优化，性能媲美有监督方法，为大模型测试时自适应提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [118] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CritICL 是一种新颖的推理时框架，利用同族较弱模型的结构化失败模式作为指导，通过评论式上下文学习提升大语言模型推理能力，同时保持高效率。包含动态和静态两种变体，实验显示其性能优于标准上下文学习，且在生成次数和 token 成本上显著低于测试时扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法通常依赖重复生成或外部验证，计算开销大。作者观察到同模型族内不同规模模型的失败模式呈现结构化规律，因而提出利用弱模型的失败模式作为指导信号，在不增加大量生成的情况下提升推理性能。

Method: 提出 CritICL 框架：首先从较弱模型中收集失败模式，构建评论式上下文示例。两种变体：CritICL-dynamic 根据输入自适应预测特定失败模式并检索对应评论；CritICL-static 使用全局失败模式画像提供稳定指导。两者均在推理阶段将评论作为上下文注入目标模型。

Result: 实验表明 CritICL 持续优于标准上下文学习，性能与测试时扩展方法相当或更优，同时所需生成次数和 token 成本显著降低。

Conclusion: CritICL 通过利用模型族内结构化的失败模式，以极低的推理开销实现了推理能力的显著提升，为高效的推理时计算扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [STREAM: An Objective-Driven and Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Industrial Energy Data Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26754)
*Zhipeng Ma,Bo Nørregaard Jørgensen,Zheng Grace Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STREAM框架，一种面向工业能源管理的目标驱动、不确定性感知的数据获取工作流，包含六个阶段并贯穿不确定性评估，通过两个工业案例验证，揭示数据可访问性不等于分析适用性，并支持透明决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业能源管理需要将能耗与设备状态、生产批次、物料流和工艺条件关联，但传统获取流程重连接存储，缺乏对信号是否满足能源绩效评估需求的验证，导致数据虽可访问但不一定适合分析。

Method: 提出STREAM框架，包含六阶段：目标规格化、技术需求、资源映射、源提取、归档元数据、迁移入库。引入阶段级工件、最小证据门控、源适用性规则、元数据模板、不确定性评分卡、案例追踪矩阵，并在全阶段评估测量、时间、上下文、处理四类不确定性。

Result: 通过铸造厂感应炉熔炼和奶酪粉干燥两个批次工艺案例验证，证明数据可访问性≠分析适用性，STREAM能支持对即时数据使用、分析限制和基础设施优先改进的透明决策。

Conclusion: STREAM实现了从目标到数据的端到端可追溯性，将不确定性评估嵌入全流程，为工业能源管理提供了一套可落地、可审计的数据获取方法论。

Abstract: Industrial energy management requires datasets that connect energy use with equipment states, production batches, material flows, and process conditions. However, conventional acquisition workflows commonly emphasize connectivity and storage without verifying whether accessible signals satisfy the requirements of a defined energy-performance assessment. This paper presents STREAM, an objective-driven and uncertainty-aware framework comprising Specification of Objectives, Technical Requirements, Resource Mapping, Extraction from Sources, Archival Metadata, and Migration to Database. STREAM is the central workflow: objective-to-data traceability is its end-to-end output, while measurement, temporal, contextual, and processing uncertainty are assessed across all six stages. Compared with the original conceptual STREAM sequence, this paper adds stage-level artifacts, minimum-evidence gates, source-suitability rules, a metadata template, an uncertainty rubric, and case-specific traceability matrices. The framework is validated through two industrial batch-process cases: induction-furnace melting in a foundry and cheese-powder drying using SCADA and production-order data. The results demonstrate that data accessibility is not equivalent to analytical suitability and show how STREAM supports transparent decisions about immediate data use, analytical restrictions, and prioritized infrastructure improvements.

</details>


### [120] [When Memory Takes Gradients: Collaborative Vector Memory for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26895)
*Hanchong Chen,Xing Tang,Lingjie Li,Xiongfeng Shan,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoVeMem（协作向量记忆），用冻结的 LightGCN 用户/物品向量替代纯文本叙事记忆，让候选集自行检索最相关的历史状态作为软令牌送入 LLM 上下文，通过对比对齐与列表级联合训练教会模型阅读向量记忆并排序，实现零额外 LLM 记忆维护调用的同时在 4 个基准上 19/20 指标达 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agentic 推荐系统用 LLM 生成的文本叙事作为持久用户记忆，存在两大瓶颈：1) 记忆只能逐次重写，利用完整交互历史成本过高；2) 协作过滤的核心证据（全目录上的梯度相似度）在翻译成自然语言时丢失。

Method: 1. 内存库构建：离线训练并冻结 LightGCN，得到用户/物品向量作为协作记忆库。2. 决策时检索：候选集自行从记忆库检索最相关历史状态，与轻量文本画像拼接为软令牌输入 LLM。3. 两阶段训练：先用物品语义锚点做对比对齐，再用掩码候选的列表级联合训练教模型读取向量记忆并排序，最后用逐点是/否读出为每个候选打分。

Result: 在 4 个指令驱动推荐基准上，CoVeMem 在 20 个指标单元中 19 个达到或超越最强文本记忆基线；记忆维护零额外 LLM 调用（基线需每交互一次），且完整交互历史变得可微、可作为训练数据。

Conclusion: 向量化协作核心记忆并让其直接参与梯度流，解决了文本记忆的扩展性与信息损耗问题，为 Agentic 推荐提供了高效、可端到端优化的新范式。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems ground each decision of a large language model (LLM) in a persistent memory of the user, and in existing agents that memory is text: a narrative written and maintained by further LLM calls. Text limits this memory in two ways. It is updated one rewrite at a time, so exploiting the full interaction history is prohibitively expensive; and collaborative evidence, graded similarity over an entire catalog, does not survive translation into sentences. We propose CoVeMem (Collaborative Vector Memory), which vectorizes the collaborative core of the agent's memory. Frozen LightGCN user and item states form the memory bank; at each decision, the candidate set itself retrieves the most relevant historical states, which enter the LLM's context as soft tokens alongside a light textual profile. Contrastive alignment to item-semantic anchors, followed by listwise co-training with masked candidates, teaches the model to read these states and to rank through them; a pointwise yes/no readout scores each candidate. Across four instruction-grounded recommendation benchmarks, CoVeMem matches or exceeds the strongest collaborative text-memory agent on 19 of 20 metric cells while requiring zero additional LLM calls for memory maintenance beyond the shared static profile, against per-interaction calls for text memory. The memory now takes gradients: the full interaction history, out of reach for text, becomes available as training data for what the agent remembers and for how it reads what it remembers.

</details>


### [121] [Topology-Masked Unified Backbone for Joint Feature Interaction and Multi-Domain Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27005)
*Zhihao Zhu,Dezheng Han,Jikang Xia,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出MaskRec，一种拓扑掩码统一token交互架构，用于大规模点击后转化率预测。该方法将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号统一为token表示，引入全局记忆token和域级记忆token作为信息聚合节点，并设计结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，根据信息源的结构差异和建模需求选择性启用或阻断注意力连接，从而在同一拓扑约束注意力过程中完成异构特征交互和多域序列建模。实验在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上验证了其优于官方基线的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业排序模型通常用独立模块分别处理异构特征交互和多域用户行为序列建模，近期的统一架构虽尝试融合但依赖模块间协调，未能在同一交互空间内充分组织所有信息源。需一种能在统一token空间内同时建模异构特征交互和多域序列依赖的方法。

Method: MaskRec将异构特征、多域行为序列和上下文信号映射为统一token表示，引入可学习的全局记忆token和域级记忆token作为信息聚合节点。基于统一token空间设计结构化注意力掩码TopoMask，根据不同信息源的结构差异和建模需求，选择性启用或阻断注意力连接。同时引入双路径交互式查询生成模块，在统一骨干网络前注入候选条件下的用户-物品交互信号。

Result: 在腾讯广告算法大赛数据集上，MaskRec相较于官方基线取得了稳定的性能提升，验证了该统一框架在工业CVR预测任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: MaskRec通过统一token表示、记忆token聚合和拓掩码注意力机制，成功将异构特征交互与多域序列建模融合在同一拓扑约束注意力过程中，并结合双路径查询生成模块注入候选感知信号，为大规模工业CVR预测提供了一个有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Large-scale post-click conversion rate (CVR) prediction requires jointly modeling heterogeneous feature interactions and dependencies over multi-domain user behavior sequences. Existing industrial ranking models usually handle these two aspects with separate modules. Recent unified architectures attempt to incorporate them into a single framework, but such unification often relies on coordination between modules and does not fully organize all information sources within the same interaction space. To address this problem, we propose MaskRec, a topology-masked unified token interaction architecture for feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling. MaskRec transforms heterogeneous features, multi-domain behavior sequences, and contextual signals into unified token representations, and further introduces learnable global memory tokens and domain-level memory tokens as information aggregation nodes. Based on this unified token space, MaskRec designs a structured attention mask, TopoMask, which selectively enables or blocks attention connections according to the structural differences and modeling requirements of different information sources. In this way, heterogeneous feature interaction and multi-domain sequence modeling are performed within the same topology-constrained attention process. In addition, MaskRec incorporates a dual-path interactive query generation module to inject candidate-conditioned user--item interaction signals before the unified backbone. Experiments on the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Competition dataset show that MaskRec achieves stable improvements over the official baseline, validating the effectiveness of the proposed unified framework for industrial CVR prediction.

</details>


### [122] [Conversational Recommendation over Live E-Commerce Catalogues with Self-Refreshing Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27006)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Dionizije Fa,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向实时电商目录的商家无关多轮对话购物助手，核心是自刷新检索器与控制器对话层，仅用LLM处理意图分类和偏好引导，检索、重排序、多样性选择由专用函数完成，实现目录增量同步与低延迟推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的对话推荐系统多在静态预索引物品集上评估，但实际电商目录持续变化（新增、下架、改价、补货），亟需能实时适应动态目录、且不依赖特定商家数据结构的对话推荐方案。

Method: 1. 自刷新检索器：摄入商家产品源，丰富记录后写入向量索引；利用逐项哈希比对识别新增、变更、删除、未变产品，仅处理增量数据避免全量重建。2. 控制器对话层：消费向量索引，LLM仅负责意图分类与偏好引导，检索、重排序、多样性选择作为独立确定性函数执行。3. 部署为WhatsApp购物助手，目录变更经下一次同步即生效。

Result: 构建了可上线的WhatsApp对话购物助手，实现目录变更准实时生效；提供在线演示机器人、完整文档与演示视频，代码开源于GitHub。

Conclusion: 该架构通过增量索引同步与职责分离（LLM管对话理解，专用模块管检索排序），在保持推荐质量的同时大幅降低计算开销与延迟，适合生产环境大规模部署，为动态电商场景下的对话推荐提供了工程化范式。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems based on large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated on static, pre-indexed item collections, yet e-commerce catalogues change continuously as products are added or removed, repriced, and restocked. We present a merchant-agnostic, multi-turn conversational shopping assistant that operates over such live catalogues. Its central component is a self-refreshing retriever that ingests a merchant product feed, enriches the records, and synchronizes them into a vector index. On each run, per-item hashes identify which products are new, changed, deleted, or unchanged, so only the delta is processed rather than rebuilding the whole catalogue. A controller-based dialogue layer consumes this index, using an LLM only for intent classification and preference elicitation while retrieval, reranking, and diversity selection run as dedicated functions. Our demonstration is a WhatsApp shopping assistant in which catalogue changes reach the recommendations after the next successful sync. A live chatbot, documentation, and a recorded walkthrough are available at https://github.com/infobip/infobip-agentic-crs.

</details>


### [123] [ProRetrieval: Learning to Orchestrate Hybrid Search via Executable Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27017)
*Chengsong You,Zhen Sun,Yunhai Hu,Junwei Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Binyu Li,Ziyan Zhao,Weiyao Wang,Liren Lu,Zhijie Ye,Yumo Cao,Yitao Long,Yiwei Xu,Qiyi Jiang,Xuanyi Fu,Yufan Chen,Yilun Li,Rongkang Xiong,Yiran Zou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ProRetrieval 将语言模型重塑为检索编排器，通过混合 DSL 交织 SQL 结构化算子与向量检索原语，利用 SQL 逻辑代数融合异构候选集，在电商与邮件基准上以 4B 参数模型超越 GPT-5.5 等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合检索管道（如互惠秩融合、自查询检索器）仅支持固定组合形式；近期强化学习检索器将语言模型仅作为单一后端的查询生成器，缺乏对异构检索路径的编排能力。

Method: 提出 ProRetrieval：语言模型合成混合 DSL 可执行程序，交织结构化字段的 SQL 算子与文本/图像的向量检索原语，SQL 提供逻辑代数融合异构候选集。采用 GRPO 与 DAPO 训练 Qwen3-4B，设计分层四项奖励函数。

Result: 在 Amazon 商品与 Enron 邮件两个新基准上，4B 模型 Hit@1 分别达 0.81 与 0.91，显著优于 GPT-5.5（0.69/0.86）、Claude Opus 4.7 及全面检索、LLM 增强、结构化查询、图基基线。

Conclusion: ProRetrieval 通过语言模型编排异构检索路径，以小模型实现最先进检索性能，验证了程序化检索编排的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world retrieval often composes structured constraints with semantic intents over text and images through arbitrary Boolean logic. Existing hybrid pipelines such as reciprocal rank fusion or self-querying retrievers admit only a fixed form of composition, while recent reinforcement-learning retrievers train the language model as a query generator for a single backend, leaving the orchestration of heterogeneous retrieval paths outside its action space. We propose ProRetrieval, which recasts the language model as a retrieval orchestrator: given a natural-language query, it synthesizes an executable program in a hybrid DSL interleaving SQL operators over structured fields with vector-retrieval primitives over text and images, with SQL itself providing the logical algebra that fuses heterogeneous candidate sets. We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO and DAPO under a hierarchical four-term reward, and evaluate on two new benchmarks built from Amazon products and Enron email. Our 4B model surpasses GPT-5.5 (Hit@1 0.81 vs. 0.69 on e-commerce; 0.91 vs. 0.86 on email) and Claude Opus 4.7 and a comprehensive suite of retrieval, LLM-augmented, structured-query, and graph-based baselines. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProRetrieval/; data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/ProRetrieval.

</details>


### [124] [Astar: Learning to Propose Evolution Directions for Self-Evolving Industrial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27287)
*Jinxin Hu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Lingyu Mu,Muyu Zou,Weiqin Yang,Sirui Chen,Bohao Wang,Zhezheng Hao,Hao Zhang,Zulong Chen,Shizhun Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Astar，一种基于训练的专用进化引导模型，解决AI系统迭代中“提出进化方向”依赖资深专家的瓶颈。通过从工业迭代历史中学习，Astar-8B在阿里巴巴Lazada广告系统实现单次提案成功率0.6786，远超人类专家(0.3229)和GPT-5.5(0.3071)，并实现了20轮全自动连续迭代，显著提升离线指标和在线业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过持续迭代推进，但“提出有效进化方向”这一起点仍高度依赖资深专家，成为关键瓶颈。通用大模型(甚至GPT-5.5)只能给出泛泛且错位的建议，因为所需专业知识源于经验积累而非显性编码，难以直接注入。

Method: 提出Astar，从工业系统的大量迭代历史中学习专用进化引导模型。针对稀疏监督、噪声数据、巨大方向空间和昂贵验证四大挑战：数据端设计管道将噪声历史提交转化为大规模洁净进化语料(成对样本扩展+噪声过滤)；模型端采用中间训练、SFT和RL训练，用层级提示引导方向生成，并用RL中的奖励模型作为快速代理评估器。

Result: Astar-8B在Lazada广告系统实测中单次提案成功率达0.6786，远超人类专家0.3229和最强通用LLM 0.3071。实现全自动闭环迭代：两周内连续指导20轮迭代，离线Hitrate@200提升23.6%，在线A/B测试GMV相对提升4.86%，广告收入相对提升1.82%。

Conclusion: Astar成功打破了AI系统迭代中“进化方向提出”的专家依赖瓶颈，通过专用模型学习工业迭代经验，实现了全自动、闭环的持续进化，且性能显著超越人类专家和通用大模型，验证了数据驱动的专用进化引导范式的有效性。

Abstract: Modern AI systems advance through continuous iteration: a loop of proposing evolution directions, implementing code, training, and evaluation. While the latter three stages are increasingly automated, the starting point --- proposing effective evolution directions --- remains a critical bottleneck that still relies heavily on senior experts. In this work, we explore whether AI can take over this role. We find that general-purpose LLMs, even the advanced GPT-5.5, offer only generic and misaligned suggestions: the required expertise is accumulated through experience rather than explicitly codified, and thus hard to inject directly.
  To this end, we propose Astar, a training-based approach that learns a specialized evolution-guiding model from the abundant iteration histories of industrial systems. Realizing this idea, however, raises four challenges: sparse supervision, noisy data, a vast direction space, and prohibitively expensive verification. We address them along two fronts. On the data side, we design a pipeline that turns noisy historical commits into a large, clean evolutionary corpus via pairwise sample expansion and noise filtering. On the model side, we train the model through mid-training, SFT, and RL, guiding evolution direction generation with hierarchical hints and using the reward model in RL as a fast surrogate evaluator.
  Astar has been deployed in Alibaba's Lazada advertising system for evolution direction proposal. Astar-8B achieves a single-proposal success rate of 0.6786 in real-execution evaluation, far exceeding human experts (0.3229) and the strongest general-purpose LLM (0.3071). More importantly, Astar closes the loop and enables fully automatic iteration: it guided 20 consecutive iterations over two weeks, improving offline Hitrate@200 by 23.6%, while an online A/B test yielded relative lifts of 4.86% in GMV and 1.82% in advertising revenue.

</details>


### [125] [Stageboost: Recommending Signals Based on Counterfactual Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27366)
*Darpan Singhal,Matan Mandelbrod,Tal Franji,Manasa Kolla,Vipul Gaba,Yuri Brovman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: eBay提出两阶段XGBoost模型优化View-Item页面信号展示，实验显示整体GMB提升0.08%，零配件类目GMB提升0.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过在商品详情页展示上下文信号（短文本/视觉片段），提供额外商品信息，促进智能购买决策并激励用户参与。

Method: 采用两阶段XGBoost模型：第一阶段预测各信号的展示价值，第二阶段优化信号组合与排序，实现View-Item页面信号的最优填充。

Result: 线上实验显示整体Gross Merchandise Bought (GMB) 提升0.08%，Parts and Accessories类目GMB提升0.58%，主要归因于高平均价格商品转化率的提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段XGBoost建模方法有效解决了信号选择与排序的联合优化问题，显著提升了商品成交额，尤其在高客单价类目效果显著，验证了智能信号展示对电商转化的商业价值。

Abstract: Signals are short textual or visual snippets displayed on the eBay View-Item (VI) page, providing additional, contextual information for users about the viewed item. The aim of displaying these signals is to facilitate intelligent purchase and to incentivize engagement. In this paper, we present a 2 stage xgboost based model that optimally populates the VI page with signals. This approach has shown a 0.08% lift in overall GMB (Gross Merchandise Bought) and 0.58% increase in Parts and Accessories GMB, primarily due to increase in conversion of high average price items in online experimentation.

</details>


### [126] [Scaling Graph Neural Networks for Friend Recommendation: Multi-Hash User Embeddings and Temporal Neighbor Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27413)
*Maksim Utushkin,Andrei Ovsiannikov,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可扩展的端到端GNN排序系统，用于生产级社交图，通过多哈希ID嵌入和时间邻居采样两大设计，显著降低存储和计算开销，并在大规模图上实现了显著的线上效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好友推荐本质上是图结构问题，依赖多跳社交上下文而非仅用户属性。在拥有数亿用户和数百亿边的生产级社交图上部署消息传递GNN面临建模和系统挑战，特别是高基数ID嵌入存储过大（>200GB）和时间邻居采样扫描全邻接表导致高度数用户计算不可行。

Method: 1) 多哈希ID嵌入作为主要节点表示，将ID嵌入表大小减少98%以上同时保持排序质量。2) 时间邻居采样采用时间戳排序的CSR存储结构配合二分查找，将单节点时间采样复杂度从O(deg(v)+k)降至O(log(deg(v))+k)。构建了分布式训练和推理框架。

Result: 在1.94亿用户、280亿边的图上，离线消融实验验证每个设计选择的贡献。线上A/B测试显示，相比强生产基线，推荐好友添加数增加16%，唯一好友添加者增加11.5%。

Conclusion: 多哈希嵌入和高效时间邻居采样的结合使得大规模时序图上的GNN部署成为可能，显著降低存储和计算成本并带来可衡量的业务收益，且已开源分布式训练推理框架。

Abstract: Friend recommendation is inherently graph-structured: the relevance of a potential connection depends on multi-hop social context rather than user attributes alone. However, deploying message-passing GNNs on a production-scale social graph with hundreds of millions of users and tens of billions of edges requires addressing numerous modeling and systems challenges. We present a scalable end-to-end GNN ranking system for production social graphs, focusing on two design choices that are critical in this setting: multi-hash ID embeddings and temporal neighbor sampling. Multi-hash embeddings are common for high-cardinality features, but industrial GNN systems typically either ignore trainable IDs or accept full embedding tables, exceeding 200 GB for our graph. We integrate multi-hash as the primary node representation, reducing the ID-embedding table size by more than 98 percent while preserving ranking quality. Temporal neighbor sampling is well understood in principle, but existing implementations scan full adjacency lists, which is a non-starter for users with tens of thousands of friends. We implement timestamp-sorted CSR storage with binary search, reducing the per-node temporal sampling cost from $O(deg(v) + k)$ to $O(\log(deg(v)) + k)$. Beyond these components, we show that this combination scales and yields measurable production impact. On a graph with 194M users and 28B edges, offline ablations isolate each design choice's contribution. In an online A/B test, our system increases friend additions from recommendations by 16 percent and unique friend adders by 11.5 percent over a strong production baseline. We release our framework for distributed training and inference on large temporal graphs.

</details>


### [127] [misi: a Metric Inverted Sample Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27422)
*Edgar Chavez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 misi，一种基于随机采样词汇表的倒排索引，用于通用度量空间中的近似最近邻搜索。该索引将每个对象表示为其 k_b 个最近采样点，通过即插即用的内层索引构建，查询采用 IDF 加权共享邻居投票加精确验证。该设计将 NAPP 索引从常数枢轴推广到线性大小的词汇表，使发帖列表长度在 n 增长时保持常数期望值 ρ = k_b/α，并将索引变为组合器：任何在 αn 个点上具有高召回率的索引都能生成 n 个点上的索引。理论分析给出了召回保证和极限。实验显示构建极快、确定性、内存占用极低（3 GiB 构建上限，8 GB 服务上限），适合频繁重建语料库、批量相似度任务、受限内存服务场景；代价是查询耗时比饱和图基线慢 6-16 倍，0.99 召回的验证预算随 n^{0.30} 增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索索引（如基于图的方法）在构建时往往需要大量内存、不可确定、难以并行化，且对频繁重建语料库、批量相似度工作负载、受限内存服务等场景不友好。需要一种构建快、确定性强、内存占用可控、支持黑盒度量空间、且易于并行化的索引结构。

Method: 1. 从数据库中随机采样大小与 n 成正比的词汇表（大小为 αn）。2. 使用即插即用的内层索引，为每个对象找到其 k_b 个最近采样点，建立倒排列表。3. 查询时，对查询向量同样找到最近采样点，利用 IDF 加权共享邻居投票选出 C 个候选，再进行精确距离验证。4. 理论分析：发帖列表期望长度 ρ = k_b/α 随 n 不变；召回保证要求 k_b 在重叠间隙上对数级增长；投票无法区分低于 1/√k_b 量级的重叠差异。

Result: 构建性能：10^8 向量在 64 核上耗时 5,250 秒，比同召回率图构建快 3.7 倍；构建流式进行，内存上限 3 GiB。服务性能：NVMe 上服务 10^8 向量，内存上限 8 GB，低于 SSD 图基线的工作底线。查询速度：饱和图基线在内存中快 6-16 倍；0.99 召回的验证预算随 n^{0.30} 增长。所有实验带种子、饱和度扫描、完整配置，包含负面结果。

Conclusion: misi 牺牲查询吞吐，换取构建速度、确定性、低内存占用和对任意度量空间的黑盒支持。适用于频繁重建语料库、批量相似度工作负载、受限内存服务等重构建成本、确定性、内存占用而非峰值吞吐的场景。

Abstract: We present misi, an inverted index for approximate nearest-neighbor search over general metric spaces whose vocabulary is a random sample of the database, of size proportional to $n$. Each object is represented by its $k_b$ nearest sample points, found by a pluggable inner index over the sample; queries are answered by an idf-weighted shared-neighbor vote followed by exact verification of $C$ candidates. The construction generalizes the NAPP index from a constant number of pivots to a linear-size vocabulary, which keeps posting lists at constant expected length $ρ= k_b/α$ as $n$ grows and turns the index into a combinator: any high-recall index on $αn$ points yields an index on $n$ points, for any metric. A probabilistic model gives a recall guarantee -- $k_b$ logarithmic in $n$ over the overlap gap suffices, with a verification budget the index itself estimates -- and a matching limit: the vote cannot resolve overlap differences below order $1/\sqrt{k_b}$. The design's strengths are structural: construction is $n$ independent searches -- embarrassingly parallel, deterministic, $5{,}250$ s for $10^8$ vectors on 64 cores, $3.7\times$ faster than a matched-recall graph build -- it streams under an enforced 3 GiB cap, and the portable artifact serves $10^8$ vectors from NVMe within an enforced 8 GB budget, below the working floor of the SSD-graph baseline. Its cost is query-time work: saturated graph baselines answer $6$-$16\times$ faster in RAM, and the verification budget for 0.99 recall grows as $n^{0.30}$. All results carry seeds, saturation sweeps and full configurations, are generated from run manifests, and include measured negative results. The intended applications weight construction cost, determinism, memory footprint, or black-box metrics over peak throughput: frequently rebuilt corpora, batch similarity workloads, constrained-memory serving.

</details>


### [128] [hoBIT: A Profile-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Chatbot for University Academic Advising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26604)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seongmin Lee,Joongheon Kim,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: proFILL 是一种将基于规则的学术咨询聊天机器人转化为档案感知 RAG 系统的方法，通过渐进式获取用户档案属性并条件化检索，显著提升咨询回答的准确性与适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学学术咨询中，相同问题因学生院系、入学批次、学位项目不同而需不同答案，现有无档案感知的检索器会返回看似合理但不适用的证据，导致咨询不准确。

Method: proFILL 根据查询意图与初始检索证据，渐进式获取仅当前查询所需的档案属性，构建档案感知索引并以此条件化检索增强生成，无需预先完整用户档案。

Result: 大量实验与人类偏好研究表明，proFILL 超越多种 RAG 基线，受目标用户偏爱，且在开源权重模型上保持有效，支持低成本本地部署。

Conclusion: proFILL 通过动态档案感知检索解决了学术咨询中的上下文依赖问题，实现了更准确、用户满意且可大规模部署的解决方案。

Abstract: In university academic advising, identical questions can require different answers depending on a student's department, admission cohort, and degree program, causing profile-blind retrievers to surface plausible but inapplicable evidence. We present proFILL, a method for transforming hoBIT, our college's current rule-based advising chatbot, into a profile-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system. Rather than requiring a complete user profile upfront, proFILL progressively acquires only the profile attributes needed for each query, guided by both the query intent and the initially retrieved evidence, and uses them to condition retrieval over a profile-aware index. Extensive experiments and a human preference study show that proFILL outperforms diverse RAG baselines, is preferred by target users, and remains effective with open-weight models for cost-effective on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [129] [Assessing the Downstream Utility of Evidence-Aware Retrieval in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26379)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究检索评估中答案支持信号对下游RAG任务的实际效用，发现其虽改变检索排序，但并不统一提升所有下游决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将检索评估与生成所需证据对齐，是否能使基于检索评估的下游决策更可靠。

Method: 在五个检索基准和端到端TREC RAG 2025设置中，考察答案支持信号在四种角色中的表现：比较检索器、指导检索训练与系统选择、预测下游答案质量、过滤提供给生成器的证据。

Result: 答案支持信号改变检索排序，但下游价值不一：不能可靠改进检索器训练；系统选择的收益取决于生成器使用证据的指令；基于该信号的检索分数不能稳健预测未见主题的答案质量；人工标注证实过滤保留有用证据段落，但不同答案评估器对答案质量提升的结论不一。

Conclusion: 使检索评估更贴近生成所需证据，本身并不保证所有下游用途更可靠；RAG评估方法应针对其预期支持的具体比较、决策和结论进行评估。

Abstract: Retrieval evaluation for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly designed around whether retrieved passages contain evidence that can support generation, rather than topical relevance alone. We study whether this closer alignment with downstream evidence needs also makes retrieval evaluation more useful for the decisions built from it.
  Across five retrieval benchmarks and an end-to-end TREC RAG 2025 setting, we examine an answer-support signal in four roles: comparing retrievers, guiding retrieval training and system selection, predicting downstream answer quality, and filtering the evidence supplied to a generator. The signal changes retrieval rankings, but its downstream value is not uniform. It does not reliably improve retriever training; the benefit of using it for system selection depends on how the generator is instructed to use the retrieved evidence; and retrieval scores based on it do not robustly predict answer quality on unseen topics. In a direct evidence intervention, human annotators confirm that filtering preferentially preserves passages containing useful answer evidence, yet different answer evaluators reach different conclusions about whether the resulting answers improve.
  These results show that making retrieval evaluation more closely reflect the evidence needed for generation does not by itself make every downstream use of that evaluation more reliable. RAG evaluation methods should therefore be assessed with respect to the particular comparisons, decisions, and conclusions they are intended to support.

</details>


### [130] [BLANC: Discovering Patent White Space via Changes in Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information Between Multi-View Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26685)
*Shuichi Miyazawa,Kensuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 BLANC（基于 NPMI 条件的空白景观分析），一种三阶段流水线，用于在专利景观中识别“白空间”（即全局已建立但局部未探索的技术组合）。方法结合多视角神经主题建模、归一化点互信息（NPMI）量化跨维度聚类关联，以及条件检测机制，引入 ΔNPMI 指标量化关键词过滤前后 NPMI 下降程度。在两个公开 USPTO 语料（机器学习/AI 与玻璃成分）上通过人工消融实验验证，BLANC 能以 34.1% 和 27.3% 的召回率恢复被消融的目标组合，且在随机消融对照中零误报；单视角基线与传统共现指标均失效。专有浮法玻璃/玻璃陶瓷专利案例中，关键词“氟”揭示了专家独立确认的氟表面处理×抗翘曲抑制候选（ΔNPMI 高达 0.48）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白空间识别方法依赖人工专利制图或单视角聚类，缺乏定量缺口检测能力，无法系统性发现‘全局已建立、局部未探索’的技术组合，阻碍战略研发规划。

Method: BLANC 三阶段流水线：1) 多视角神经主题建模，沿应用/用途、新颖性、创造性步骤三个语义维度建模；2) 使用归一化点互信息（NPMI）量化跨维度聚类间的关联强度；3) 条件检测：按用户指定关键词过滤语料，计算过滤前后 NPMI 差值 ΔNPMI，标记 ΔNPMI 显著下降的组合为白空间候选。

Result: 在机器学习/AI（5,417 件专利）与玻璃成分（1,982 件专利）语料上，消融目标组合 75% 文档后，BLANC 分别以 34.1% 和 27.3% 召回率恢复目标；191 次诱饵试验（随机消融或消融其他组合）中目标从未被恢复（零误报）。单视角合并基线召回率为 0，传统共现指标在随机消融下也会触发，缺乏特异性。专有案例（302 件浮法玻璃/玻璃陶瓷专利）中，关键词‘氟’揭示氟表面处理×抗翘曲抑制候选，ΔNPMI 高达 0.48，经专家独立确认。

Conclusion: BLANC 通过多视角语义建模与 ΔNPMI 条件检测，实现了对专利景观中白空间的定量、可解释识别，优于现有基线且具备零误报特性，可辅助战略研发决策。

Abstract: Identifying white space --- the unexplored but potentially valuable regions of a patent landscape --- is essential for strategic R&D planning, yet existing methods rely on manual patent mapping or apply single-view clustering without quantitative gap detection. We propose BLANC (Blank Landscape Analysis through NPMI Conditioning), a three-phase pipeline combining (1) multi-view neural topic modeling along three semantic dimensions (application/use, novelty, inventive step); (2) Normalized Pointwise Mutual Information (NPMI) to quantify cross-dimensional cluster association; and (3) conditional detection that flags combinations whose NPMI drops when the corpus is filtered by a user-specified keyword. The drop is captured by a new metric, $Δ$NPMI, which identifies combinations "established globally, unexplored locally." Because white space has no ground truth, we evaluate BLANC on two public USPTO corpora --- machine learning/AI (5,417 patents, CPC G06N) and glass compositions (1,982 patents, CPC C03C) --- by artificially depleting known technology combinations and testing recovery. When three-quarters of a target pair's documents are removed, BLANC recovers 34.1% (ML/AI) and 27.3% (glass) of the depleted combinations, whereas size-matched removals not aimed at them (random documents, or those of a different established combination) essentially never do: the target is never recovered in 191 decoy trials. Collapsing the three semantic views into one recovers nothing, while prior co-occurrence measures also flag the target under random removal, offering no specificity. In a proprietary case (302 float glass / glass-ceramics patents), the keyword "fluorine" reveals a fluorine surface treatment $\times$ warpage suppression candidate ($Δ$NPMI up to 0.48) that experts had independently identified.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing的架构，将小语言模型（SLM）直接集成到图消息选择中，解决传统图神经网络将属性表示为静态特征向量、无法根据预测目标动态选择语义证据的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络通常将标签属性图中的异构文本和类别属性表示为静态特征向量，这限制了模型根据特定预测目标动态决定哪些语义证据应影响消息传播的能力。需要一种机制能够将语言模型的语义理解能力融入到属性丰富的图学习中，实现目标条件下的消息选择和路由。

Method: 提出SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing架构：1) 使用拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示和预测锚点；2) 对每个目标节点，将邻居结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型组合为消息；3) 参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软标记，生成目标条件路由查询；4) 查询先在每种关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系类型摘要路由信息；5) 最终表示为拓扑锚点提供有界残差更新，保留结构证据同时允许语义信息修正预测。

Result: 该架构在邻居级和关系类型级支持可解释性分析，提供了一种将语言衍生语义集成到属性丰富图学习的通用机制，能够在保持结构证据稳定性的同时，利用上下文语义信息动态调整预测。

Conclusion: 通过将小语言模型集成到图消息传播的选择和路由过程中，该方法实现了结构表示与语义理解的有效结合，为处理异构属性丰富的图数据提供了新的范式，兼具预测性能提升和可解释性。}

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [132] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一种白盒模糊测试框架，利用LLM内部安全神经元作为连续执行反馈，构建SafetyOracle将神经元激活转为连续安全警报分数，实现无需生成响应即可评估越狱攻击有效性，并在21个模型上实现高越狱发现率和零样本迁移攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化安全测试方法依赖响应级反馈，需生成目标模型响应来评估攻击效果，成本高且对强对齐模型仅提供稀疏指导（多数候选被同等拒绝），缺乏细粒度反馈。

Method: NeuronFuzz利用模板不变的有害和良性输入及稳定性感知选择识别紧凑安全神经元集，构建SafetyOracle将激活转为连续安全警报分数（预填充阶段即可获取）。利用该分数可微分性，计算梯度识别安全敏感模板位置，结合掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，保留原始有害载荷且无需额外优化变量。

Result: 在5个白盒源模型上实现76%-100%越狱发现率，超越基线最多48个百分点。优化模板零样本迁移至开源权重及6个专有目标模型，平均ASR达69.6%/92.6%（top-5集成EASR），专有模型平均ASR 44.1%/60.0%（top-5集成EASR）。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz通过白盒神经元级反馈显著提升LLM安全评估效率与效果，实现高越狱发现率并展示跨模型强迁移攻击能力，为LLM安全评估提供新范式。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [133] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于PyTorch的研究框架，用于设计和优化二值化神经网络，并引入了一种新颖的剪枝方法，该方法通过考虑不同抽象层级参数的相对重要性，在保持模型精度不变的情况下，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率，显著优于现有方法的41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的剪枝策略不适合二值化表示，且很难转化为实际的硬件节省。虽然二值化与剪枝结合有望带来额外的效率提升，但缺乏有效的方法来同时优化两者。

Method: 开发了一个包含冻结和剪枝机制的PyTorch框架，用于快速评估和原型设计二值化神经网络；提出了一种基于全局加权机制的新颖剪枝方法，该方法考虑了不同抽象层级学习参数的相对重要性。

Result: 在VGG11上，该方法在保持精度不变的情况下实现了70%的剪枝率，而最先进方法在二值化设置下仅达到41%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架和剪枝方法为二值化神经网络的进一步压缩提供了有效工具，实现了更优的精度与剪枝率权衡，有助于在受限边缘硬件上部署高效模型。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [134] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限步Newton-Schulz迭代在Muon优化器中不仅不损害收敛保证，反而能通过平滑奇异值映射，使Muon在非光滑非凸优化中实现对驻点的收敛，且样本复杂度达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要么用精确极性分解替代Newton-Schulz迭代，要么将有限迭代视为近似误差，认为只会损害收敛保证。本文旨在证明有限步Newton-Schulz迭代实际上对非光滑非凸优化有益。

Method: 采用online-to-nonconvex转换框架，将Muon更新规则视为在线学习器，通过遗憾界转换为驻点保证。分析有限步Newton-Schulz迭代如何将不连续的极性映射平滑为奇异值的Lipschitz映射，构建平滑谱势函数。

Result: 证明Newton-Schulz迭代深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证收敛到驻点，而精确极性更新可能不收敛。样本复杂度界匹配非光滑非凸优化最佳已知保证，且对光滑非凸优化在问题相关因子下最优。结论推广到具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Conclusion: 有限步Newton-Schulz迭代通过平滑奇异值映射，使Muon在非光滑非凸优化中获得理论收敛保证，这一机制超越特定算法，适用于更广泛的谱映射族。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [135] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMELS 是一种利用代数多重网格求解器加速标签传播的高效框架，解决了大规模高维数据集上的计算和内存瓶颈，实现了快速且鲁棒的半监督标签传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工标注大规模数据集成本高昂且耗时，标签传播作为一种半监督学习技术虽能从少量标签传播信息到大量无标签数据，但受限于计算开销和内存约束，难以应用于大规模高维数据集。

Method: 提出 AMELS 框架：结合快速邻域图构建与代数多重网格（AMG）求解器，用 AMG 迭代求解器替代传统标签传播中的随机游走迭代，利用多层级特性在单个多重网格循环中完成任意规模图上的标签信息传播。

Result: 与现有实现相比显著降低运行时间，且对超参数选择更具鲁棒性（运行时间和分类准确率均表现稳定），在大规模图像数据集上仅需少量标签样本即可产生准确标签。

Conclusion: AMELS 实现了大规模数据集上的高效标签传播，在标签极度稀疏条件下仍能获得准确分类结果，为半监督学习在大规模场景下的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [136] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两种私有最佳N采样方法（PrivBoN和PrivITP），通过添加校准噪声同时解决奖励黑客和隐私保护问题，实现差分隐私和KL正则化对齐，且在隐私预算超过临界阈值时无额外对齐成本，PrivITP进一步解耦正则化参数和隐私参数，实验验证其扩展单调性且优于BoN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoN采样虽然简单但存在奖励黑客（利用代理奖励模型误差）和缺乏隐私保护（敏感人类偏好数据无保护）两大问题，需要一种统一干预同时解决两者。

Method: 提出PrivBoN：添加Gumbel噪声实现ε-差分隐私和KL正则化对齐；提出PrivITP：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样和两阶段高斯机制，实现事后(ε,δ)-DP，隐私成本与响应数n无关，解耦正则化参数和隐私参数。

Result: 理论证明PrivBoN在隐私预算超过ε*时达到信息论天际线；PrivITP达到天际线直到噪声膨胀项。实验显示两者均具有扩展单调性，PrivITP在强隐私下优于PrivBoN。

Conclusion: 单一噪声添加干预同时解决奖励黑客和隐私保护，PrivITP提供更灵活且性能更优的私有对齐方案，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpEmbed 框架，利用结构化、隐私保护的支持工单元数据（不使用工单文本），为云端 LLM 服务学习紧凑的操作指纹。通过八通道操作签名、时序对比学习、跨视图重构和代际序数正则化，在 Google Cloud 33,000+ 生产工单、7 个 LLM 系列、26 个月数据上验证，能恢复可解释的家族/版本级结构，显著提升留一模型外操作预测，支持早期窗口数据稀疏场景及跨模型故障类型迁移，为模型上线、支持就绪评估和运维监控提供实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管 LLM 服务已进入生产系统，但模型选择与服务规划仍严重依赖能力基准测试，这些基准难以反映部署后的实际运行行为。需要一种能从生产环境运维数据中学习服务操作特征的方法，以指导模型上线、支持准备度评估和持续运维监控。

Method: 1. 将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名（结构化、隐私保护的工单元数据，不含文本）。2. 通过时序对比学习、跨视图重构、代际序数正则化三大目标联合训练，学习低维操作嵌入表示。3. 在 Google Cloud 真实生产环境（33,000+ 工单、7 个 LLM 家族、26 个月）上进行大规模评估。

Result: OpEmbed 能恢复清晰的家族级和版本级可解释结构；在留一模型外操作预测任务上显著优于非学习基线；在早期窗口数据有限时仍保持有效性；支持跨模型故障类型迁移。实验验证了其在模型上线、支持就绪评估、运维监控等实际场景的实用价值。

Conclusion: OpEmbed 证明了从结构化运维元数据中学习紧凑操作指纹的可行性与有效性，填补了能力基准与生产运行行为之间的鸿沟，为 LLM 云服务的全生命周期运维决策提供了数据驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [138] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CG4AI框架，通过凸组合模型并强制线性约束，用主LP确定权重、定价子问题生成新模型、割平面扩展可行性，在MNIST分类和多商品流路由问题上实验表明能生成可行预测器且优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练仅最小化损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义规则或约束；实际应用（自主系统、网络路由等）需要此类保证。

Method: CG4AI框架：构建AI模型的凸组合并强制线性约束；主线性规划（LP）确定最优混合权重；定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦最违反约束；割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。应用于MNIST数字分类（四种约束用法）和多商品流问题（链路容量约束）。

Result: 在MNIST和SNDLIB基准网络上实验表明，CG4AI能可靠产生可行预测器，且准确率优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI提供了一种有效框架，将约束整合到模型组合中，实现了可行性保证和性能提升，适用于需要硬约束的实际应用。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [139] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“潜在诊断分类法”框架，通过维度优化分类器、提取潜在支持向量及攻击幅度构建诊断分类，将提示词分为四类处理区域（安全依赖、启发式偏差、启发式覆盖、上下文不足），并在提示注入数据集上验证：约77%高置信度决策对单token移除不鲁棒，分解为置信度校准失效与可利用捷径两类失效模式，并给出各区域修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器在高置信度决策下仍存在脆弱性，缺乏系统性诊断工具识别哪些决策可信、哪些需人工介入，且嵌入维度常固定而非数据驱动优化，导致模型鲁棒性评估不足。

Method: 1. 维度优化分类器：通过交叉验证实证选择嵌入维度而非预设固定值。2. 定位潜在支持向量：提取约29%训练样本作为关键支持向量，识别改变预测标签的关键token及其攻击幅度。3. 构建诊断分类法：基于token敏感性与攻击幅度将提示词划分为四类处理区域，提供端到端处理指南。

Result: 在公开提示注入数据集上，约77%高置信度决策对单token移除不鲁棒；脆弱性分解为两类：置信度校准失效（模型过度自信但易翻转）与真实可利用捷径（存在可系统利用的模式）。各分类区域给出针对性修复策略。

Conclusion: 潜在诊断分类法为分类器决策提供可解释的安全保障层，揭示高置信度决策的系统性脆弱性，并给出分区域修复路径，适用于提示注入等安全关键场景的模型诊断与加固。
